If none are available it waits up to timeout\n142\t// for new ones to arrive (or until ctx is cancelled).\n143\tfunc (p *Poller) TakeUpdates(ctx context.Context, timeout time.Duration) []contract.Update {\n144\t\tp.mu.Lock()\n145\t\tif len(p.updates) > 0 {\n146\t\t\tout := p.updates\n147\t\t\tp.updates = nil\n148\t\t\tp.mu.Unlock()\n149\t\t\treturn out\n150\t\t}\n151\t\tp.mu.Unlock()\n152\t\n153\t\tselect {\n154\t\tcase <-p.newData:\n155\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n156\t\t\treturn nil\n157\t\tcase <-time.After(timeout):\n158\t\t\treturn nil\n159\t\t}\n160\t\n161\t\tp.mu.Lock()\n162\t\tout := p.updates\n163\t\tp.updates = nil\n164\t\tp.mu.Unlock()\n165\t\treturn out\n166\t}\n167\t\n168\t// Health returns the current health status of the poller.\n169\tfunc (p *Poller) Health() contract.HealthResponse {\n170\t\tp.mu.Lock()\n171\t\tdefer p.mu.Unlock()\n172\t\treturn contract.HealthResponse{\n173\t\t\tOK:              p.polling,\n174\t\t\tPolling:         p.polling,\n175\t\t\tLastUpdateID:    p.lastID,\n176\t\t\tUptimeSeconds:   int64(time.Since(p.started).Seconds()),\n177\t\t\tContractVersion: contract.ContractVersion,\n178\t\t\tVersion:         p.version,\n179\t\t\tCommitSHA:       p.commitSHA,\n180\t\t}\n181\t}\n182\t\n183\t// offsetFile represents the JSON structure of the offset file.\n184\ttype offsetFile struct {\n185\t\tOffset int64 `json:\"offset\"`\n186\t}\n187\t\n188\t// loadOffset reads the persisted offset from disk. /file/{file_id}.\n329\t// It calls Telegram's getFile to resolve the file_path, then streams the bytes from\n330\t// the Telegram file CDN. Returns 404 for invalid/expired file_id, 413 if > 20MB.\n331\tfunc handleFile(s *telegram.Sender) http.HandlerFunc {\n332\t\treturn func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n333\t\t\tfileID := r.PathValue(\"file_id\")\n334\t\t\tif fileID == \"\" {\n335\t\t\t\twriteProxyError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, 400, \"missing file_id\")\n336\t\t\t\treturn\n337\t\t\t}\n338\t\n339\t\t\ttgFile, apiErr := s.GetFile(r.Context(), fileID)\n340\t\t\tif apiErr != nil {\n341\t\t\t\t// Telegram 400-range errors mean invalid or expired file_id.\n342\t\t\t\tif apiErr.ErrorCode >= 400 && apiErr.ErrorCode < 500 {\n343\t\t\t\t\twriteProxyError(w, http.StatusNotFound, 404, \"file not found or expired\")\n344\t\t\t\t\treturn\n345\t\t\t\t}\n346\t\t\t\twriteTelegramError(w, apiErr)\n347\t\t\t\treturn\n348\t\t\t}\n349\t\n350\t\t\tif tgFile.FilePath == nil {\n351\t\t\t\twriteProxyError(w, http.StatusNotFound, 404, \"file not available\")\n352\t\t\t\treturn\n353\t\t\t}\n354\t\n355\t\t\tif tgFile.FileSize != nil && *tgFile.FileSize > telegram.MaxFileSize {\n356\t\t\t\twriteProxyError(w, http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge, 413, \"file exceeds 20MB limit\")\n357\t\t\t\treturn\n358\t\t\t}\n359\t\n360\t\t\tresp, err := s.DownloadFile(r.Context(), *tgFile.FilePath)\n361\t\t\tif err != nil {\n362\t\t\t\twriteProxyError(w, http.StatusBadGateway, 502, fmt.Sprintf(\"download failed: %v\", err))\n363\t\t\t\treturn\n364\t\t\t}\n365\t\t\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n366\t\n367\t\t\tif resp.StatusCode == http.StatusNotFound {\n368\t\t\t\twriteProxyError(w, http.StatusNotFound, 404, \"file not found or expired\")\n369\t\t\t\treturn\n370\t\t\t}\n371\t\t\tif resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n372\t\t\t\twriteProxyError(w, http.StatusBadGateway, 502, fmt.Sprintf(\"Telegram returned %d\", resp.StatusCode))\n373\t\t\t\treturn\n374\t\t\t}\n375\t\n376\t\t\tct := resp.Header.Get(\"Content-Type\")\n377\t\t\tif ct == \"\" {\n383\t\t\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Length\", cl)\n384\t\t\t}\n385\t\n386\t\t\tfilename := path.Base(*tgFile.FilePath)\n387\t\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Disposition\", fmt.Sprintf(`attachment; filename=%q`, filename))\n388\t\n389\t\t\tif _, err := io.Copy(w, resp.Body); err != nil {\n390\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"file stream %s: %v\", fileID, err)\n391\t\t\t}\n392\t\t}\n393\t}\n394\t\n395\tconst (\n396\t\tmaxPhotoSize    = 10 * 1024 * 1024 // 10MB\n397\t\tmaxMediaSize    = 50 * 1024 * 1024 // 50MB for document/audio/video\n398\t\tmultipartMemory = 32 * 1024 * 1024 // max memory for multipart parsing (rest goes to disk)\n399\t)\n400\t\n401\t// parseMediaForm parses a multipart form with the given size limit and returns the\n402\t// required chat_id. Returns false and writes an error if parsing or chat_id fails.\n403\tfunc parseMediaForm(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, maxSize int64) (chatID int64, ok bool) {\n404\t\tr.Body = http.MaxBytesReader(w, r.Body, maxSize+4096) // +4096 for form fields\n405\t\tif err := r.ParseMultipartForm(multipartMemory); err != nil {\n406\t\t\tvar maxErr *http.MaxBytesError\n407\t\t\tif errors.As(err, &maxErr) {\n408\t\t\t\twriteProxyError(w, http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge, 413, fmt.Sprintf(\"file exceeds %dMB limit\", maxSize/1024/1024))\n409\t\t\t} else {\n410\t\t\t\twriteProxyError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, 400, \"invalid multipart form: \"+err.Error())\n411\t\t\t}\n412\t\t\treturn 0, false\n413\t\t}\n414\t\tchatIDStr := r.FormValue(\"chat_id\")\n415\t\tchatID, err := strconv.ParseInt(chatIDStr, 10, 64)\n416\t\tif err != nil || chatID == 0 {\n417\t\t\twriteProxyError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, 400, \"invalid or missing chat_id\")\n418\t\t\treturn 0, false\n419\t\t}\n420\t\treturn chatID, true\n421\t}\n422\t\n423\t// optionalInt64 parses a form value as int64, returning nil if absent.\n424\tfunc optionalInt64(r *http.Request, key string) *int64 {\n425\t\tif v := r.FormValue(key); v != \"\" {\n436\t\t\tif n, err := strconv.Atoi(v); err == nil {\n437\t\t\t\treturn &n\n438\t\t\t}\n439\t\t}\n440\t\treturn nil\n441\t}\n442\t\n443\t// optionalStr returns a pointer to the form value if non-empty, else nil.\n444\tfunc optionalStr(r *http.Request, key string) *string {\n445\t\tif v := r.FormValue(key);
